### [76] [EmergencyBias: Bias in Text-to-Image Models under Emergency Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00598)
*Haibo Tang,Linqi Zhang,Hongxin Huan,Chenwei Lin,Xian Xu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文定义了EmergencyBias——一种在应急场景下文本到图像（T2I）生成中的偏见，涵盖人口统计偏见和行为偏见两个方面。通过评估框架对7个主流T2I模型、6种应急场景和3个人口统计维度进行系统研究，发现T2I模型在应急场景中存在显著的人口统计偏见和行为偏见（尤其体现于性别维度），并提出ActionAlign方法——一种轻量级提示嵌入校准方法，能够有效减少行为偏见同时保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I偏见研究主要关注静态、显式的偏见形式（如性别、种族、地理文化在人物呈现上的差异），而忽略了行为偏见——即不同群体如何在行动、反应和社会角色分配中被刻画。应急场景提供了一个揭示此类偏见的理想设置，因为它要求模型不仅描绘"谁在场"，还要描绘"谁处于风险中、谁进行干预、责任如何分配"。

Method: 作者定义EmergencyBias（包含人口统计偏见和行为偏见），构建系统化评估框架，对7个领先T2I模型、6种代表性应急场景和3个人口统计维度（性别、年龄、肤色）进行测试。提出ActionAlign——一种轻量级提示嵌入校准方法，用于减少行为偏见。评估包括空白提示（无人口统计限定）和受控提示（限定人口统计特征）两种条件。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）在无人口统计限定的空白提示下，T2I模型在应急场景中表现出明显的人口统计偏见，体现在性别、年龄和肤色分布上；（2）在受控提示下，行为偏见系统性地与人口统计变化相关联，性别差异尤为突出，且不同模型间存在显著差异；（3）ActionAlign在减少行为差异方面优于代表性基于提示的基线方法，同时基本保持图像质量。

Conclusion: 应急场景是T2I模型偏见评估的重要设置，本研究提供了在具有社会后果的场景中实现更公平视觉生成的实际方向。ActionAlign作为一种实用的偏见校准方法，展示了在无需大幅影响图像质量的前提下改善T2I生成公平性的可行性。

Abstract: Bias in Text-to-Image (T2I) generation has become an important problem in multimedia content creation and communication. However, existing studies have primarily focused on relatively static and explicit forms of bias, such as disparities in the representation of gender, race, and geo-cultural attributes. Less attention has been paid to behavioral bias in how different groups are portrayed acting, reacting, and occupying social roles. Emergency scenarios provide a revealing setting for studying such bias because they require models to depict not only who is present, but also who is at risk, who intervenes, and how responsibility is allocated. In this paper, we define EmergencyBias, a form of bias in T2I generation under emergency scenarios that includes both demographic bias and behavioral bias. We construct an evaluation framework to systematically study EmergencyBias across seven leading T2I models, six representative emergency scenarios, and three demographic dimensions. Our experimental results reveal three main findings. First, under blank prompts without demographic specification, T2I models exhibit clear demographic bias in emergency scenarios, reflected in the distributions of portrayed individuals across gender, age, and skin tone. Second, under controlled prompts, behavioral bias in emergency responses remains systematically associated with demographic variation, with particularly pronounced disparities along gender and substantial differences across models. Third, we introduce ActionAlign, a lightweight prompt-embedding calibration method that outperforms a representative prompt-based baseline in reducing behavioral disparities while largely preserving image quality. Overall, our work identifies emergency scenarios as an important setting for bias evaluation in T2I models and offers a practical direction toward fairer visual generation in socially consequential contexts.

</details>


### [77] [FATE: Frame-Level Audio-Visual Temporal Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01310)
*Kaisi Guan,Bingzi Zhang,Xihua Wang,Ying Ba,Xin Cheng,Yijing Chen,Ruihua Song*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出FATE（帧级音视频时序嵌入）模型，通过保留帧级序列并沿物理时间轴对齐，结合跨视频语义对比学习和视频内时序对比学习的联合目标，同时捕捉音频-视觉内容中的语义与时间对齐信息，在时序和语义检索、零样本事件定位及生成评估指标上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频模型在语义和时间对齐两方面难以兼顾：嵌入模型能匹配语义但丢失时间信息，同步模型能捕捉时间偏移但缺乏语义理解。作者希望构建同时具备两种能力的表征。

Method: FATE保留每个模态的帧级序列而非池化为单一嵌入，在物理时间轴上对齐帧对并计算严格对齐帧间的相似度；训练时采用联合目标，结合跨视频语义对比学习和视频内时序对比学习，将同步信息编码到可复用的嵌入空间中。

Result: 在三个任务中，FATE在时序和语义检索上大幅超越最强基线；在零样本事件定位中达到与全监督方法相当的性能；作为生成评估指标时与人类判断的相关性最高。

Conclusion: FATE成功弥合了嵌入式模型与同步模型之间的鸿沟，证明帧级保留和对齐策略能够同时捕捉"声音是什么"和"声音何时发生"两方面的信息，在多项音视频任务中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: When a dog opens its mouth and barks, humans naturally recognize what the sound is and when it occurs. Building audio-visual models with this same ability requires representations that capture both semantic and temporal alignment. Current approaches fall short on one side or the other: embedding models match semantic but lose temporal information; synchronization models capture temporal offsets but lack semantic understanding. To bridge this gap, we propose FATE, Frame-level Audio-visual Temporal Embedding. Unlike prior embedding models that pool each modality into a single embedding and discard temporal information, FATE retains frame-level sequences, aligns them on the physical timeline, and computes similarity over strictly aligned frame pairs. Unlike synchronization models that output only an offset prediction, FATE encodes synchronization in a reusable embedding space, trained with a joint objective combining cross-video semantic and within-video temporal contrastive learning to capture both what sounds and when it occurs. Across three tasks, FATE surpasses the strongest baseline on temporal and semantic retrieval by a large margin, matches fully supervised methods on event localization in a zero-shot setting, and achieves the best correlation with human judgments as a generation evaluation metric. The source code can be found at \texttt{https://github.com/guankaisi/FATE}.

</details>


### [78] [Hear, Invoke, and Understand: A Skill-Calling Multimodal Agent for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01881)
*Yuwen Wang,Tian-Hao Zhang,Minghao Cai,Yilin Ren,Ziyang Jiang,Xin Wang,Zhichao Wang,Pan Zhou,Kun Zhan,Xinyuan Qian*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeechAgent-R，一个支持工具交互式音频推理的智能体，配合构建的HIU-Corpus数据集（65,492条交互轨迹、507.6小时音频、24个任务、8种技能、9种工具）和HIU-Bench基准（1,395个样本、56个任务），通过轨迹级监督微调结合多轮强化学习训练，在ID任务上达到84.17、OOD任务上达到70.94的分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂的声学问题可能需要模型执行声学操作、与外部工具交互并从文本或处理后的音频观测中推理，而非直接从固定音频输入给出答案。现有研究缺乏对这类工具交互式音频推理问题的系统研究。

Method: 构建HIU-Corpus数据集（含24个任务、8种技能、9种工具）；SpeechAgent-R通过两阶段训练：先进行轨迹级监督微调学习结构化交互行为，再进行多轮强化学习优化决策能力；引入HIU-Bench基准进行任务表现、交互质量和泛化性的联合评估，包含ID和OOD两个分裂。

Result: SpeechAgent-R在ID任务上达到84.17分，在OOD任务上达到70.94分，相比同一智能体框架下的基础模型分别提升了15.40和14.23分。

Conclusion: 学习技能与工具的协调能力能够显著提升音频智能体处理多样化任务设置和自适应工具交互的能力。

Abstract: Complex acoustic problems may require models to perform acoustic operations, interact with external tools and reason over the resulting textual or processed-audio observations rather than answer directly from a fixed audio input. We study such problems as tool-interactive audio reasoning and develop SpeechAgent-R, an audio agent that coordinates its intrinsic multimodal understanding with external skills and tools. To support this capability, we construct HIU-Corpus, comprising 65,492 interaction trajectories and 507.6 hours of audio across 24 tasks, 8 skills and 9 tools. SpeechAgent-R first learns structured interaction behaviors through trajectory-based supervised fine-tuning and then improves its decisions through multi-turn reinforcement learning. We further introduce HIU-Bench to jointly evaluate task performance, interaction quality and generalization to diverse task settings. It contains 1,395 samples across 56 tasks, including in-distribution (ID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) splits with substantial shifts in tool usage and workflow composition. SpeechAgent-R achieves 84.17 on ID tasks and 70.94 on OOD tasks, improving over the base model under the same agent harness by 15.40 and 14.23 points. These results demonstrate that learning skill and tool coordination improves audio agents' ability to handle diverse task settings and adaptive tool interactions.

</details>


### [79] [AcoustiTrace: When Plausible Sound Violates Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02035)
*Shiyang Li,Yuewen Cao,Yihao Liu,Yuandong Pu,Baochang Zhang,Xiaofei Li,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出AcoustiTrace，一个用于诊断音频-视频生成中声学物理真实性的基准测试框架，围绕声音生成、传播环境和声学接收八个维度进行评估，并构建了大规模数据集和验证评估器。实验表明即使领先的生成模型在基本声学过程上仍存在困难，而AcoustiTrace提供的诊断结果可指导模型优化以提升声学物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的音视频生成器虽然能产生语义合理且表面同步的声音，但常违反可见事件和环境所隐含的声学物理过程。现有基准缺乏对特定声学过程违规的归因能力和严重程度量化手段，难以支撑系统性的诊断与改进。

Method: 提出AcoustiTrace诊断基准，将文本到音视频（T2AV）和图像到音视频（I2AV）的评估围绕声学过程组织，涵盖声音生成、传播环境和声学接收八个维度，并基于可测量的声学量进行定义。构建大规模数据集（包含真实音视频记录和带声学标注的RGB-D观测），开发定向提示套件和验证过的评估器。

Result: 实验表明，即使领先的生成模型在产生看似合理的声音事件的同时，仍难以处理基本的声学物理过程（如传播环境和声学接收等方面）。AcoustiTrace针对特定声学关系的诊断结果可以有效指导模型精细化改进。

Conclusion: AcoustiTrace为音视频生成提供了系统性的声学物理真实性诊断能力，其诊断结果可引导模型向更符合物理规律的音频方向改进，并为将声学原理融入训练目标、奖励建模和候选选择开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Recent audio-video generators can produce semantically plausible and apparently synchronized sound, yet may still violate the acoustic processes implied by visible events and environments. Existing benchmarks provide limited support for attributing such violations to particular acoustic processes and quantifying their severity. We introduce AcoustiTrace, a diagnostic benchmark that formalizes acoustic physical realism in audio-video generation. AcoustiTrace organizes text-to-audio-video (T2AV) and image-to-audio-video (I2AV) evaluation around the acoustic process, covering sound generation, propagation environment, and acoustic reception through eight dimensions grounded in measurable acoustic quantities. Based on these evaluation dimensions, we construct a large-scale dataset organized around acoustic mechanisms, comprising real-world audio-video recordings and acoustically annotated RGB-D observations, and use it to develop targeted prompt suites and validated evaluators. Experiments reveal that even leading generators still struggle with fundamental acoustic processes despite producing plausible sound events. Finally, we show that the diagnostics AcoustiTrace provides for specific acoustic relations can guide model refinement toward more physically faithful audio and open new directions for incorporating acoustic principles into training objectives, reward modeling, and candidate selection.

</details>


### [80] [Estimating SSIM from MSE for DCT-Based Compressed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02549)
*Luc Trudeau,Maria G. Martini*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从全局PSNR/MSE近似计算SSIM的方法，利用仅从参考图像中提取的局部统计信息（基于方差或标准差加权）来重分配全局MSE，从而在JPEG压缩图像中无需访问局部MSE即可准确近似SSIM，并自然扩展到视频场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SSIM计算通常需要局部MSE信息，但在DCT压缩图像的许多实际场景中（如快速编码、流媒体系统）可能无法直接获得局部MSE，因此需要一种仅依赖全局PSNR/MSE和参考图像统计信息的高效近似方法。

Method: 提出两种方法将全局MSE重分配为局部MSE近似值：(1)基于方差的加权重分配；(2)基于标准差的重分配。利用参考图像的局部统计信息计算权重，再结合全局MSE推导出SSIM近似值。

Result: 在Kodak和Xiph Subset1数据集上，跨多个JPEG质量级别进行实验，两种方法均能提供准确且稳健的SSIM近似，显著优于全局MSE基线。

Conclusion: 该框架证明SSIM可以从全局PSNR/MSE结合参考统计信息高效近似，且设计上可自然扩展至视频场景，其中参考统计信息可在同一内容的多次编码中摊销复用。

Abstract: Efficient and perceptually meaningful quality assessment is a fundamental requirement for image and video processing, compression, and streaming systems. This article shows that, in the context of Discrete Cosine Transform ( DCT)-based compressed images, Structural Similarity Index ( SSIM ) can be approximated from global Peak Signal to Noise Ratio (PSNR) or Mean Square Error ( MSE) using local statistics derived only from the reference image. While prior work assumes access to local MSE, we propose two approaches to approximate local MSE by redistributing the global MSE using variance or standard-deviation-based weighting. Experiments on the Kodak and Xiph Subset1 datasets across a range of JPEG quality levels demonstrate that both approaches provide accurate and robust SSIM approximations, substantially outperforming the global MSE baseline. The proposed framework is designed to extend naturally to video, where reference-derived statistics can be amortized across multiple encodes of the same content.

</details>


### [81] [A Brief Overview about D-Profile of Ginga DTV Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02565)
*Marcelo F. Moreno,Debora C. Muchaluat-Saade,Guido Lemos de Souza Filho,Raoni Kulesza,Alan L. V. Guedes*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西数字电视（DTV）系统标准的最新修订，重点阐述了Ginga中间件中D-profile接收器规范的进展及其支持广播与宽带服务深度融合的新用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西DTV标准需要演进以支持广播与宽带服务的新融合用例，但同时不能中断现有服务，因为巴西及许多ISDB-T国家仍处于模拟向数字的切换过程中。

Method: 选择中间件层作为增量演进的切入点，基于Ginga这一开放平台及其接收器配置文件体系，在巴西DTV论坛主导下由学术界牵头协作制定D-profile Ginga DTV接收器规范。

Result: Ginga D-profile接收器规范的制定取得了显著成果，有效支持了广播与宽带深度融合的新用例，且未对现有DTV服务造成干扰。

Conclusion: 基于接收器配置文件的中间件层次化演进策略是有效的，Ginga D-profile为巴西DTV系统向混合广播宽带服务过渡提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The Brazilian DTV system standards have been recently revised in order to address new use cases related to deeper integration be- tween broadcast services and broadband services. Such an evolution could not disrupt the current DTV services since Brazil and many ISDB-T countries are still under the switch-off process from ana- log to digital. The middleware layer is the best candidate for such an incremental (yet powerful) evolution. In the case of Ginga, the Brazilian DTV middleware, as an open platform based on receiver profiles, the results were impressive. In this paper, we focus on the advances and new use cases addressed by the D-profile Ginga DTV receiver specification carried out in the Brazilian DTV Forum under a collaborative effort led by the Academia

</details>

### [82] [Uncertainty-Aware Simulation-Based Inference for Operations Research with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00019)
*Liang Guo,Lin Shaochong,Shen Zuo-Jun Max,Zhang Kun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的不确定性感知推理框架，通过短视前瞻模拟和重要性重采样来改进大语言模型在运筹学数学建模任务中的生成质量，在多个基准上超越标准基线和低温基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在运筹学（OR）任务中部署时，正确的数学建模过程比最终答案更重要，但标准自回归生成采用短视策略，无法预见局部步骤是否能扩展为全局一致的优化模型，导致局部看似合理的步骤可能传播为严重的下游公式或求解器代码错误。

Method: 提出一种不确定性感知、无需训练（training-free）的推理框架：不更新模型参数，使用短前瞻模拟（short lookahead simulations）评估中间候选步骤的下游预测不确定性或概率集中度，再通过重要性重采样（importance resampling）动态选择更可能产生连贯数学公式的候选步骤。

Result: 在多个OR基准（包括NL4OPT、MAMO和IndustryOR）上的实验表明，该框架一致优于标准基线和低温采样基线，建立了高效且无需训练的可靠OR公式生成范式。

Conclusion: 该工作证明无需任何参数更新，仅通过推理阶段的不确定性感知策略即可显著提升LLM在运筹学建模任务中的可靠性，为OR公式生成提供了一种高效、训练自由的替代方案。整体来看，这是一篇针对LLM在运筹学具体任务中推理缺陷的改进研究。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) for operations research (OR) tasks remains challenging because correctness depends on a coherent modeling process, not merely a correct final answer. Standard autoregressive generation operates on a myopic policy, which sometimes fails to anticipate whether a partial formulation can be validly extended into a globally consistent optimization model. Consequently, locally plausible steps may propagate into catastrophic downstream formulation or solver code errors. To address this, we propose an uncertainty-aware, training-free inference framework for OR mathematical modeling. Without updating model parameters, our method evaluates intermediate candidate steps using short lookahead simulations to quantify downstream predictive uncertainty or probability concentration. Candidates that demonstrate a higher likelihood of yielding coherent mathematical formulations are then dynamically selected via importance resampling. Empirical evaluations across multiple OR benchmarks (including NL4OPT, MAMO, and IndustryOR) demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms both standard and low-temperature baselines, establishing an efficient, training-free paradigm for reliable OR formulation generation.

</details>


### [83] [Learning Compositional Meta-Routing for Agentic Workflows: An Executable Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00106)
*Natan Vidra,Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可执行的智能体路由基准测试和一个预算感知的元路由器，能够从原始任务文本中组合异构操作（如检索、代码执行、委派等）。在保留测试集上，学习策略达到100%成功率，比静态流程降低成本43%，但在词汇偏移挑战集上泛化能力下降，揭示词汇泛化是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由工作大多孤立地选择模型端点、检索深度或工具，缺乏从原始任务文本出发组合异构操作的系统性方法，智能体系统需要决定"先做什么操作、再做什么操作"而非仅选择答案。

Method: 构建包含216训练/72开发/108保留测试/108词汇偏移挑战任务的基准测试；采用独立正则化逻辑回归头从字符和词特征预测操作概率，经温度缩放后，在路线成本和动作数量预算下进行贪心组合编排。

Result: 保留测试集上学习策略100%成功（静态工作流93.5%），成本降低43%；单次路由器仅56.5%。词汇偏移挑战集上学习成功率降至75.9%，落后于静态路由93.5%，但仍便宜49%且比单次路由高34.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 词汇泛化而非路线执行是主要限制；本文建立了可复现的测试平台和有界的概念验证，但结果不代表真实LLM性能。

Abstract: Agentic systems must decide not only what answer to produce, but which reasoning and execution operations should precede it. A controller may answer directly, decompose a request, retrieve evidence, execute code, delegate to a specialist, or verify an intermediate result. Existing routing work largely selects model endpoints, retrieval depth, or tools in isolation. We introduce an executable benchmark and a budget-aware meta-router that composes heterogeneous operations from raw task text. The benchmark contains 216 training, 72 development, 108 held-out test, and 108 locked lexical-shift challenge tasks across data analysis, frozen-corpus research, and document processing. Outcomes are machine checked after operations execute. Independent regularized logistic heads predict operation probabilities from word and character features, are temperature-scaled on development data, and are greedily composed under route-cost and action-count budgets. On the held-out test, the learned policy achieves 100% success versus 93.5% for strong static and fixed workflows, with 43% lower cost than the static policy; a matched learned one-shot router reaches 56.5%. On the untouched challenge split, learned success falls to 75.9% and trails static routing at 93.5%, while remaining 49% cheaper and exceeding one-shot routing by 34.3 points. The gap identifies lexical generalization, rather than route execution, as the principal limitation. These results establish a reproducible testbed and a bounded proof of concept, not evidence of live-LLM performance.

</details>


### [84] [MetaRoute-Bench: Evaluating Meta-Decision Policies for Agentic Workflow Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00107)
*Natan Vidra,Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MetaRoute-Bench，一个用于比较智能体元决策（如直接回答、分解任务、调用工具、委派、验证等路由策略）的开源可检查基准框架。该基准包含180个合成任务、8种路由策略和30个随机种子，在43,200条追踪轨迹上，任务感知的组合策略达到79.4%的成功率，优于静态策略（76.7%）、一次性任务路由（67.4%）和直接回答（52.9%），但代价是更高的成本和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统在运行时需要反复做出元决策（直接回答、分解任务、调用工具、执行代码、委派专家、验证结果或从失败中恢复），这些决策影响任务成功率、成本和延迟，但现有评估方法往往将决策嵌入编排框架中，仅通过总体任务准确率来评估，缺乏对元决策策略的系统性比较。

Method: 构建MetaRoute-Bench开源基准框架，包含180个涵盖数据分析、研究、文档处理的合成任务画像，8种路由策略，30个配对随机种子，基于离线执行模型生成43,200条可检查的追踪轨迹，并对各策略的成功率、成本、延迟进行统计比较和消融分析。

Result: 任务感知的组合策略达到79.4%成功率，优于静态策略76.7%、一次性任务路由67.4%和直接回答52.9%。与静态策略相比提升2.7个百分点（95%置信区间±2.0），但成本增加4.7%、延迟增加6.4%。消融实验显示，限制路由组合为单一操作或移除验证机制时损失最大。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献是提供了一个可复现的评估方法和路由策略权衡分析框架，而非生产环境有效性的证据。研究结果基于种子化的离线执行模型而非实时部署，作者发布了任务生成、策略、轨迹、测试和分析工件以支持在线系统验证。

Abstract: Agentic systems must repeatedly decide whether to answer directly, decompose a task, invoke a tool, execute code, delegate to a specialist, verify an intermediate result, or recover from failure. These meta-decisions affect not only task success but also operating cost and latency, yet they are often embedded inside an orchestration framework and evaluated only through aggregate task accuracy. We present MetaRoute-Bench, an open, inspectable framework for comparing meta-decision policies under a shared execution model. The initial benchmark contains 180 synthetic task profiles spanning data analysis, research, and document processing, eight routing policies, and 30 paired random seeds. Across 43,200 traces, a task-aware compositional policy achieves 79.4% success compared with 76.7% for a strong workload-specific static policy, 67.4% for one-shot task routing, and 52.9% for direct answering. Relative to the static policy, this is a 2.7 percentage-point improvement with paired 95% CI of plus or minus 2.0 points, at 4.7% higher mean cost and 6.4% higher latency. Ablations show the largest losses when route composition is restricted to one operation and when verification is removed. These results are generated by a seeded offline execution model rather than a live deployment; accordingly, the primary contribution is a reproducible evaluation method and an analysis of routing-policy tradeoffs, not evidence of production effectiveness. We release task generation, policies, traces, tests, and analysis artifacts to support live-system validation.

</details>


### [85] [Progressive$^2$: A Teacher-Student Progressive Co-Evolving Knowledge Distillation Method for Substantial Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00129)
*Tiancong Cheng,Ying Zhang,Zhiwen Yu,Yifang Yin,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Progressive²的新型知识蒸馏方法，通过"渐进增强的教师"和"渐进缩小的学生"相结合的方式，解决教师与学生能力差距过大时蒸馏性能严重下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏在服务端模型压缩中广泛应用，但当服务器模型能力与客户端需求之间存在巨大差距时，蒸馏性能会显著下降。现有方法未能有效解决这一大能力差距场景下的蒸馏瓶颈问题。

Method: Progressive²包含两个核心策略：教师侧按"从粗糙到精细"的语义进程逐步选择更多层参与蒸馏，形成系统化学习课程，并设计基于Lipschitz连续性理论支持的多特征融合适配器提升训练稳定性；学生侧通过逐步缩小网络规模实现与教师的迭代协同进化。该框架灵活，教师渐进策略可独立使用，教师与学生联合集成可进一步提升性能。

Result: Progressive²作为灵活框架，教师侧渐进策略可单独部署以在精度与训练效率之间取得最优平衡，教师与学生联合集成可带来整体性能的进一步提升。

Conclusion: Progressive²通过渐进式教师和学生协同进化，有效缓解了大能力差距下的蒸馏性能退化问题，并提供了灵活可配置的框架设计。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) is a widely utilized technique for transferring knowledge from a large model (the teacher) to a smaller model (the student). Owing to its flexibility and broad applicability, KD has been extensively applied in the compression of server-side models to meet the Quality of Service (QoS) requirements of client users. Despite significant advancements, the performance of distillation is substantially compromised when a large disparity exists between the capabilities of the server and the requirements of the client. To alleviate this problem, we propose a novel distillation approach, named Progressive$^2$, which operates through the combination of a progressively stronger teacher and a progressively smaller student. On the side of the teacher, rather than involving all layers simultaneously, we progressively select additional layers for distillation following a raw-to-rich semantic progression, establishing a systematic learning curriculum. Furthermore, we design a teacher-side multi-feature fusion adapter for the teacher to improve training stability, which is theoretically supported by the framework of Lipschitz continuity. On the side of the student, rather than directly training a tiny model, we gradually reduce the size of the network to facilitate an iterative co-evolution with the teacher. Progressive$^2$ serves as a flexible framework; the progressive strategy of the teacher can be deployed independently to achieve an optimal balance between accuracy and training efficiency, while the joint integration of the teacher and the student yields further improvements in overall performance.

</details>


### [86] [Rethinking Pretraining for Specialized Design Data: Evidence from the JONES-19 Cultural Design Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00135)
*Alexandros Haridis,Charles Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于JONES-19设计图像数据集，比较了CNN在ImageNet预训练与从头训练两种策略下的判别性能，发现从头训练配合多尺度局部采样可以弥补预训练带来的性能提升，表明在专业设计领域，精心策划的小型高质量数据集可能比大规模通用预训练更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计与建筑档案以图像格式编码专家知识，为设计启发的机器学习提供了重要测试平台，但现有研究对CNN在该领域的训练策略缺乏系统评估。

Method: 基于JONES-19数据集（来源于《装饰语法》，1857年），评估CNN在两种训练策略下的判别性能：(a) ImageNet预训练迁移学习，(b) 在JONES-19上从头训练，并引入多裁剪重复局部采样增强。

Result: 领域通用的预训练先验能提升判别性能，但从头训练结合多裁剪局部采样可以有效弥补这些性能差距；对于高度结构化的设计数据，局部设计驱动的表示足以支撑学习。

Conclusion: 在专业设计领域，精心整理的小型高质量数据集可能比大规模数据收集更具效力和信息量，不应过度依赖大规模通用预训练。

Abstract: Design and architectural archives encode expert human knowledge in graphical formats, providing a critical testbed for design-inspired Machine Learning (ML) challenges absent with typical computer vision benchmarks. Building on JONES-19, a small-size image dataset based on The Grammar of Ornament (London, 1857), we evaluate the discriminative performance of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) in two model training strategies: (a) ImageNet pretraining for domain-general "visual common sense," and (b) learning from scratch on the design data in JONES-19. We find that while domain-general priors improve discriminative performance, learning from scratch augmented with repeated local sampling (multi-crop) effectively recovers these gains. For highly structured design data, local design-driven representations provide sufficient foundation for learning, challenging a reliance on massive general-purpose pretraining. These findings suggest that in specialized design domains, careful curation of smaller high-quality datasets that capture empirical and formal design principles may prove more effective and informative on the nature of a particular design domain than prioritizing large-scale data collection.

</details>


### [87] [Leak It: A Probabilistic Approach to Training-Data Extraction from Black-Box Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00144)
*Victor Maricato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过概率视角研究语言模型的成员推断攻击（MIA），指出聚合ROC-AUC指标具有误导性——盲目基线甚至能达到AUC 0.97。作者提出逐文档提取审计方法，发现模型确实会逐字提取训练数据中的真实标识符（如邮箱），泄露率随模型规模增长（410M到6.9B从5.6%增至16.6%），且代码领域泄露风险约为散文的3倍。去重和采样参数影响甚微。据此建议隐私审计应报告逐文档、分领域的提取情况而非单一AUC，并发布leakit审计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIA评估使用聚合ROC-AUC指标存在根本性缺陷：模型无关的盲目基线仅凭表面文本就能区分成员与非成员，导致AUC被虚高，掩盖了真实的数据泄露风险。作者希望从概率视角重新审视采样式黑盒泄露，并提出能反映真实危害的逐文档审计方法。

Method: 将N次采样视为输出分布p(.|x)的估计，把泄露信号形式化为该分布的函数。在WikiMIA和IID Pile（MIMIR）上对比盲基线，验证采样信号是否带来增量。进一步提出逐文档提取审计：检查给定文档的独特标识符（如邮箱）是否能从其前缀条件下被精确恢复，并通过不匹配前缀对照组归因泄露来源。

Result: 盲袋词分类器在WikiMIA上达AUC 0.97且采样无增益；MIMIR上自浓度和黄金续写恢复均不显著优于盲基线。但逐文档层面，Pythia-6.9B上83/500（16.6%）含真实标识符的文档被精确提取，泄露随模型规模增长（5.6%→16.6%），代码领域泄露强度约为散文3倍，散文仍正相关（4.0%→12.1%）。温度、核采样影响小，16 token前缀即可触发，去重无缓解作用。

Conclusion: 聚合AUC指标无法反映真实隐私危害，隐私审计应转向逐文档、分领域的提取评估。作者发布leakit工具供黑盒提取审计使用，并强调去重不能替代提取防护。

Abstract: Membership inference (MIA) on language models is usually summarised by an aggregate ROC-AUC, but such evaluations are confounded: model-free blind baselines separate members from non-members from surface text alone. We study black-box, sampling-based training-data leakage through a probabilistic lens, treating N samples from p(.|x) as an estimate of the output distribution and casting leakage signals as functionals of it. We extend the blind-baseline critique into the sampling regime: on WikiMIA a blind bag-of-words classifier reaches AUC 0.97 (TPR 0.90 at 5% FPR) and sampling adds nothing, while on an IID Pile split (MIMIR) neither self-concentration nor gold-continuation recovery significantly beats a blind baseline (incremental AUC 95% CI includes zero). Aggregate metrics hide the real harm. The same sampling verbatim-extracts training data for a tail of documents no blind attack can reach. On Pythia-6.9B, 83 of 500 Pile documents bearing a real identifier (16.6%; 21.3% of those bearing an email address) have that exact identifier reproduced AND not reproduced under a mismatched-prefix control, so each leak is attributable to that document, not to a globally common string. This per-document disclosure is invisible to aggregate AUC and grows with capacity (5.6% to 16.6% from 410M to 6.9B). The risk is uneven: identifier leakage is ~3x stronger in code than prose, though prose stays clearly positive and also grows with capacity (4.0% to 12.1%), while recovery of arbitrary held-out continuations is confined to code (+0.44 member gap on GitHub vs at most +0.014 on prose). Temperature and nucleus sampling matter little, a 16-token prefix suffices, and we detect no reduction from corpus deduplication. Privacy audits should report per-document extraction, decomposed by domain, not a single AUC. We release leakit, a black-box extraction-audit tool.

</details>


### [88] [Response Magnitude as a Dominant Signal for Held-Out CRISPRi Perturbation Effect Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00152)
*Mehrdad Shoeibi,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了CRISPRi扰动转录组效应预测问题，发现目标基因的安德森-达林距离可由四个确定性标量函数高度预测，线性回归即可超越深度MLP编码器，且幅度预测器在零样本跨细胞类型迁移中表现优于表达预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单细胞生物学中，预测CRISPRi扰动对目标基因转录组效应的大小是一个重要的开放问题。近期研究表明简单基线方法往往能超越深度扰动预测器，本文旨在理解这一现象背后的低维信号及其跨细胞类型迁移特性。

Method: 在Virtual Cell Challenge基准的严格留出目标基因划分下，识别驱动表现差距的低维信号——即目标为对数安德森-达林距离，可由2000维输入的四个确定性标量函数预测。比较了深度MLP编码器、线性回归、随机森林等多种方法，并通过两个外部CRISPRi筛选实验进行零样本迁移验证。

Result: 四个幅度标量上的线性回归超越了最强的仅输入经典模型；随机森林在输入+四标量上显著优于深度编码器。幅度预测器在零样本迁移中正向迁移，而表达预测器负向或未解决。暴露幅度信息给深度编码器可改善迁移，但仍未超越四标量线性回归。

Conclusion: CRISPRi扰动幅度可被少量标量特征有效捕获，深度模型并未在特定设置下提供额外优势；跨细胞类型迁移中幅度信号更可靠，但安德森-达林距离测量的是转录组响应广度而非目标基因效应强度，这暗示迁移评估需更精确的终点定义。

Abstract: Predicting the magnitude of a CRISPRi perturbation's transcriptomic effect on held-out target genes is an important open problem in single-cell biology. Recent work has documented that simple baselines often match or exceed deep perturbation predictors on related protocols. We study this phenomenon on the Virtual Cell Challenge (VCC) benchmark under a strict held-out target-gene split, identify the specific low-dimensional signal that drives the gap, and characterize how it transfers across cell types. The target is the log Anderson-Darling distance from non-targeting controls, which is strongly predictable from four deterministic scalar functions of the 2,000-dimensional input. A deep MLP encoder with direct access to the full input collapses toward the marginal training mean, and standard remedies do not close the gap. A linear regression on the four magnitude scalars alone exceeds the strongest x-only classical model, while a Random Forest on the input plus the four scalars substantially outperforms our deep proof-of-concept encoder. Two pre-specified controls attribute the magnitude gain to per-row alignment rather than added dimensionality. Under zero-shot transfer to two external CRISPRi screens evaluated against a target-gene endpoint rebuilt from single-cell data, magnitude-only predictors transfer positively whereas expression-only predictors are negative or unresolved. Exposing magnitude to the deep encoder improves transfer over its expression-only counterpart, yet the encoder does not outperform a four-scalar linear regression on the same features. We also find that the Anderson-Darling column distributed with these screens measures transcriptome-wide response breadth rather than target-gene effect strength, so evaluating transfer against it scores a different outcome.

</details>


### [89] [Inference-Time Policy Alignment for Fair Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00175)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Conor Wallace,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种推理时策略塑形框架，通过乘法方式调整动作概率，在不需要重新训练基础策略的情况下，将预训练的深度强化学习策略引导向基于福利的公平性目标，同时保持核心任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度强化学习智能体在部署后策略僵化，难以适应新的性能标准（如公平性偏好）。传统方法假设偏好预先已知且需要完全重新训练策略，成本高昂。受大语言模型推理时对齐的启发，作者希望在不更新基础策略参数的前提下实现公平性对齐。

Method: 将推理时公平性对齐形式化为策略塑形问题，提出乘法策略塑形框架，利用动作相关的福利评分调整动作概率，无需修改基础策略参数，且与任意深度强化学习智能体兼容。

Result: 在多个领域的广泛实验中，推理时策略塑形显著改善了基于福利的公平性目标，同时保持了核心任务性能。

Conclusion: 推理时策略塑形是一种通用且有效的方法，能够在部署阶段灵活地调整预训练强化学习策略以满足公平性等新偏好，避免了昂贵的重新训练过程。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents achieve strong performance by optimizing scalar reward functions. However, once deployed, the policies of these RL agents are often rigid and costly to adapt to new performance criteria. For instance, an agent trained to maximize expected cumulative reward may not accommodate previously unknown stakeholder preferences. Existing approaches to achieve fairness, a type of preference, in RL typically assume that such preferences are known a priori and require complete retraining of the policy under a fairness-oriented metric. Inspired by inference-time alignment in large language models, we investigate the problem of steering a pretrained RL policy toward welfare-based fairness objectives at inference time without updating the base policy's parameters. We formalize inference-time fairness alignment as a policy shaping problem and propose a multiplicative policy shaping framework that adjusts action probabilities using action-dependent welfare scores, thus requiring no modification to the base policy. Our framework is general and compatible with any deep RL agent. Through extensive experiments across multiple domains, we demonstrate that inference-time policy shaping substantially improves welfare-based fairness objectives while preserving core task performance.

</details>


### [90] [AutoCause: A Python framework that automates expert decisions in environmental time-series causal discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00198)
*Marco Ruiz,Miguel Arana-Catania,David R. Ardila,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoCause是一个开源Python工作流，用于统一环境时间序列因果发现中的专家决策流程。它记录每一步决策、基于因果审计模块提供默认参数并支持领域知识覆盖，集成了三种因果发现家族的四种方法及非因果参考模型，通过方法计数支持度对链接进行分级。在145个数据集上的评估表明，多数方法支持的链接在合成基准上比单一方法更精确，但河流拓扑数据上未见优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境时间序列因果发现中，方法选择、条件独立性检验、滞后窗口、样本量、多重检验控制和证据解释等决策在不同数据集上的应用不一致，导致生成的因果图无法比较、复现和审计。

Method: 构建AutoCause工作流：记录每个决策过程；从扩展的因果审计模块推导默认参数；允许领域专家覆盖；集成三个家族共四种已建立的因果发现方法；加入非因果参考模型；根据方法计数支持度对链接分级。在145个数据集（DGP-Atlas、TimeGraph和基于拓扑的CausalRivers参考）上评估。

Result: 四种方法在合成基准上恢复了参考图的不同部分；多数方法支持的链接在合成基准上比单一方法链接更精确，但在河流拓扑数据上并未表现出同样优势。

Conclusion: AutoCause将不一致的专家实践转化为可审计、可重复的分析流程；因果解释仍由分析人员负责。

Abstract: Environmental time-series causal discovery requires expert decisions about method choice, conditional-independence tests, lag horizons, sample-size adequacy, multiple-testing control, and evidence interpretation. Applied inconsistently across datasets, these choices yield graphs that cannot be compared, reproduced, or audited. We present AutoCause, an open-source Python workflow that records each decision, derives defaults from an extended causal-audit module, and admits domain-informed overrides. The workflow wraps four established causal-discovery methods from three families, adds non-causal reference models, and grades links by method-count support. On 145 datasets from DGP-Atlas, TimeGraph, and a topology-derived CausalRivers reference, the methods recover complementary parts of the reference graphs. Majority-supported links are more precise than single-method links on the synthetic benchmarks but not against river topology. AutoCause converts inconsistent expert practice into an auditable, repeatable analysis; causal interpretation remains with the analyst. Available at https://github.com/marcoruizrueda/autocause.

</details>


### [91] [A Physics-Chemistry-Informed Neural Network (PCINN) for Real-Time Spatial-ALD Coverage Prediction and Reliable Kinetics Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00212)
*Ning Hu,Chang Liu,Yunlei Jiang,Yuan Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理-化学信息神经网络（PCINN），作为空间原子层沉积（SALD）的高保真代理模型，以约7ms的实时速度达到CFD级精度（测试R²=0.998），仅需30个训练样本。网络只学习操作条件到近壁浓度闭合，表面动力学为硬编码可训练化学层，同时进行了完整的可辨识性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SALD是工业原子层沉积的关键技术，但设计控制受限于表面覆盖率的预测成本：高保真CFD太慢，无法用于操作窗口扫描，而解析模型又忽略了气体幕帘等传输调制效应，缺少兼顾速度与精度的建模方法。

Method: 构建混合代理模型PCINN：小型神经网络仅学习操作条件到近壁浓度的映射，表面动力学作为硬编码的可训练化学层沿基底轨迹积分，形成单标量瓶颈。通过Fisher信息和轮廓似然进行可辨识性分析，并推导Arrhenius参数退化谷的解析表达式。

Result: PCINN查询仅需约7ms，比CFD快约5×10⁴倍；30个训练样本即达测试R²=0.998（留一法R²=0.974），覆盖四个数量级。吸附能E_ads和脱附速率k_des可稳健辨识，k_ads在单温度下不可独立辨识；四温度下前指数因子ν和E_ads沿0.065 eV/decade退化谷耦合。

Conclusion: PCINN实现了实时、准确且可解释的SALD代理建模，可辨识性框架揭示了参数辨识的边界。退化谷斜率偏离可作为未建模位点异质性的诊断标志。数据源于仿真自洽验证，非真实参数标定。

Abstract: Spatial atomic layer deposition (SALD) is a leading atmospheric-pressure, high-throughput route to industrial ALD, but design and control are limited by the cost of predicting surface coverage: high-fidelity CFD is far too slow for operating-window scans, while analytic models miss transport modulation such as the gas curtain. We present a physics-chemistry-informed neural network (PCINN), a hybrid surrogate with CFD-level accuracy at real-time speed: a query returns coverage in about 7 ms, roughly 5x10^4 times faster than a CFD solve, reaching a test R^2_log = 0.998 (leave-one-out R^2_raw = 0.974) from only 30 training cases spanning four orders of magnitude in coverage.
  The architecture is not a black box: a small network learns only the operating-condition to near-wall concentration closure, while the known surface kinetics is a hard-coded, trainable chemistry layer integrated along the substrate trajectory. This single-scalar bottleneck keeps it accurate under sparse data, interpretable and invertible.
  We add a full identifiability analysis (Fisher information, profile likelihood). The adsorption energy E_ads and desorption rate k_des are robustly identifiable; k_ads is not separately identifiable at a single temperature (only k_ads*c_wall is). Across four temperatures the prefactor nu and E_ads bind along a weakly identifiable degeneracy valley of slope 0.065 eV/decade, derived analytically as k_B T_eff ln(10) and turned into a reliability diagnostic: a seven-chemistry mismatch matrix shows it is invariant under any single-Arrhenius mismatch and shifts only when a second thermally activated process appears, so a slope departure flags unmodelled site heterogeneity.
  Data come from simulation with known ground truth inverted by the same kinetic form, so the study verifies pipeline self-consistency and the identifiability boundary, not real parameters.

</details>


### [92] [Verifier-Induced Support Reshaping in On-Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00220)
*Shaohang Wei,Zikun Su,Feifan Song,Wen Luo,Wei Li,Guangyue Peng,Houfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个重要问题：带可验证奖励的在线强化学习（RLVR）在提升当前任务目标的同时，可能使后续目标所需的成功行为变得过于稀有而无法采样和强化，这种现象被称为"验证器诱导的支持重塑"。实验表明数学RLVR虽然提高了指令遵循的成功率，但减少了重复采样下能有成功响应的提示数量，两种任务间的能力支持发生了此消彼长的偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习通常被认为是不断提升模型能力的通用方法，但作者发现其优化方向可能只针对单一验证器，导致跨任务能力支持被重塑甚至破坏，影响后续训练的可训练性和联合能力。作者希望通过系统研究揭示这一机制。

Method: 使用两种模型家族，通过重复验证器评分采样和双向训练，在数学推理和约束指令遵循两个任务上进行实验，包括使用相反验证器进行顺序训练。采用token分布分析和受控开局干预来定位变化的集中位置，并测试参考策略约束、路由先验和在线蒸馏等缓解方法。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B-Base的IFEval上，数学RLVR使pass@1提升6.5个百分点，但best@32下降9.8个百分点；指令遵循RLVR则使数学回答从逐步解答转向直接答案，降低所有采样预算下的best@k。变化集中在响应开头几个token，RLVR主要重排基础策略已有的开局，且选定的开局因果性地影响数学搜索能力。现有缓解方法只能部分保留跨任务支持。

Conclusion: 端点改进不能保证未来的可训练性或多任务联合能力。RLVR在优化单一目标时可能损害模型在其他任务上的"可采样成功支持"，这对顺序多任务强化学习设置提出了重要警示。

Abstract: We show that on-policy reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can improve the current objective while making successful behaviors for later objectives too rare to sample and reinforce. We call this verifier-induced support reshaping and define effective rewardable support as successful trajectories reachable within a fixed rollout budget. Across two model families, we study this effect through repeated verifier-scored sampling and bidirectional training on mathematical reasoning and constrained instruction following, including sequential training with the opposite verifier. Math-RLVR raises average instruction-following success but reduces the number of prompts with any successful response under repeated sampling. On IFEval with Qwen3-8B-Base, pass@1 rises by 6.5 percentage points while best@32 falls by 9.8 percentage points, and the same divergence appears across both models and IF benchmarks. Conversely, IF-RLVR shifts math responses from step-by-step openings toward direct answers, lowers best@k across sampling budgets, and reduces reward variation for later Math-RLVR. Token-distribution analyses and controlled opening interventions show that these changes concentrate in the first few response tokens. RLVR mainly reranks openings already available in the base policy, and the selected opening causally affects math searchability. The tested reference-policy constraints, routing priors, and on-policy distillation preserve cross-task support only partially; MathIF and ReasonIF show that marginal gains translate only partly into responses that are both correct and constraint-following. Therefore, endpoint improvements do not guarantee future trainability or joint capability under on-policy optimization. Code is available at https://github.com/sylvain-wei/verifier-induced-support-reshaping

</details>


### [93] [Similarity-Aware Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00246)
*Madhavan Citalamangalam Kumaran,Midhun Parakkal Unni,Vicky Kouni,Haripriya Harikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保留感知的定位方法用于机器遗忘，同时考虑参数对遗忘数据和保留数据的重要性，并引入基于余弦相似度的保留相似评估集来直接衡量附带损伤，在CIFAR-10和ResNet18上的十一个实验设置中一致地减少了附带损伤并改善了遗忘指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于定位的机器遗忘方法仅根据遗忘集的重要性选择参数，忽视了参数在保留数据集中的作用，往往对语义相似的保留样本造成附带损伤。

Method: 提出保留感知的定位方法，同时考虑参数对遗忘数据和保留数据的重要性；并构建基于模型嵌入空间中余弦相似度的保留相似评估集来衡量附带损伤。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集和ResNet18模型上的十一个实验设置中，该方法一致地减少附带损伤并改善标准遗忘指标。

Conclusion: 保留感知的定位方法对相似性感知的机器遗忘是有效的，能够在不牺牲遗忘性能的前提下减少对相似保留样本的伤害。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of user-specified training examples from a trained model, avoiding the need to retrain it from scratch. Localization-based methods improve unlearning efficiency by identifying a subset of influential model parameters. However, existing approaches select parameters based solely on forget-set importance, neglecting their role in retained dataset and often causing collateral damage to semantically similar retained examples. We address this limitation with a retain-aware localization method that considers parameter importance to both forgotten and retained data. We also introduce a retain-similar evaluation set, constructed using cosine similarity in the model embedding space, to directly measure collateral damage. Across eleven experimental settings on CIFAR-10 dataset and ResNet18 model, our method consistently reduces collateral damage while improving standard unlearning metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of retain-aware localization for similarity-aware machine unlearning.

</details>


### [94] [Stabilized Best-of-$K$ Training for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00296)
*Melveena Jolly,Midhun Xavier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Leader Reward算法的一个窄扩展：将其二元领导者/非领导者区分替换为基于采样预算K的稳定排名信号。在固定POMO架构、3050轮训练计划和TSP-100测试集下，通过3个配对训练种子的独立采样实验，发现稳定化K=8方案在Best-of-8成本上优于原方法（7.7944 vs 7.8136），但该观察仅在估计层面且局限于特定解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leader Reward通过强调重复推理产生的最佳轨迹来修改POMO训练，本文旨在探索一个窄范围扩展——用稳定的排名信号替代二元的领导者/非领导者分类，并检验其在独立采样下的性能表现。

Method: 在POMO架构下固定3050轮训练计划和TSP-100测试集，将Leader Reward的二元判别替换为受采样预算K索引的稳定排名信号。采用3个配对训练种子，在独立采样协议下对比K=8稳定化方案与原方法的Best-of-8成本。

Result: Leader Reward复现结果在100-start、8-augmentation贪婪解码下为7.7662，与报告的7.766一致。在独立采样下，K=8稳定化配方在全部3个配对种子中均降低了Best-of-8成本（7.7944对比7.8136）。但该方法在K=1采样下弱于原方法，且在原始augmented-greedy协议下原方法仍略优。

Conclusion: 该观察仅为估计层面且具解码器特异性：3个种子低于6个种子的测试下限，Leader Reward在K=1采样下表现更好，在其原始augmented-greedy协议下仍略优。作者明确声明不对无偏估计量、普适优越性或最先进性能提出主张。

Abstract: Leader Reward modifies POMO training to emphasize the best trajectory produced by repeated inference. We test a narrow extension: replace its binary leader/non-leader distinction with a stabilized rank signal indexed by a sampling budget $K$. With the POMO architecture, 3,050-epoch schedule, and TSP-100 test set held fixed, the Leader Reward reimplementation obtains $7.7662$ under 100-start, 8-augmentation greedy decoding, matching the reported $7.766$ at its displayed precision. Under independent sampling, the stabilized $K=8$ recipe lowers realized Best-of-8 cost in all three paired training seeds: $7.7944$ versus $7.8136$. This observation is estimation-only and decoder-specific: three seeds are below the six-seed testing floor, Leader Reward is better at sampled $K=1$, and it remains slightly better under its original augmented-greedy protocol. We make no unbiased-estimator, universal superiority, or state-of-the-art claim.

</details>


### [95] [Abstention as an Action Can Kill Both the Reward Gradient and the KL Anchor: Collapse Law and Repair for Error-Penalized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00301)
*Xujun Che,Yuchen Yuan,Weida Zhao,Chenyang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在错误惩罚评分规则（答对+1，答错-λ，弃权0）下，KL锚定的梯度学习器可能反直觉地走向"拒绝一切"的退化行为：门控饱和因子同时扼杀奖励梯度和锚定恢复力，导致模型覆盖率崩溃而平均训练奖励却看似提升（以1/t趋近于零）。此外，优势估计器在稀疏回答场景下会悄悄将设计惩罚替换为有效惩罚1，使学习阈值从t*=λ/(1+λ)漂移到1/2。修复方案是结构性的：训练时强制输出置信度报告（使用严格适当评分加正确性奖励），仅在部署时通过阈值化该报告来决定是否弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误惩罚评分规则被广泛推荐用于对抗幻觉，但作者发现该规则下梯度学习可能产生与预期完全相反的行为——模型反而学会拒绝回答一切问题。理解这一失败机制对设计可靠的防幻觉系统至关重要。

Method: 通过理论分析证明KL锚定梯度学习器在离散弃权动作下的收敛行为；揭示门控饱和因子如何同时消灭奖励梯度和锚定恢复力；分析优势估计器中组归一化对有效惩罚的扭曲；提出报告级训练方案（强制置信度报告 + 严格适当评分 + 正确性奖励）作为结构修复。

Result: 仿真验证了所有理论预测；在两种规模的语言模型实验中确认了机制的实际运作：该规则能在十步优化器内让模型沉默其仍能正确解答的问题；消融实验隔离了原因；报告级训练同时提升了覆盖率、准确率和校准度。

Conclusion: 基于错误惩罚的弃权训练在KL锚定梯度学习中存在根本性缺陷，不应直接用作防幻觉训练规则；正确做法是将置信度报告的校准训练与部署时的阈值化弃权决策分离，从而避免门控饱和带来的梯度死亡问题。

Abstract: Error-penalized scoring rules ($+1$ for a correct answer, $-λ$ for a wrong one, $0$ for abstaining) are increasingly prescribed against hallucination: a rational agent facing such a rule answers exactly when its correctness probability exceeds Chow's threshold $t^\ast=λ/(1+λ)$. We prove that a KL-anchored gradient learner can do the opposite. When abstention is a discrete action, the reward gradient and the anchor's restoring force are throttled by the same gate-saturation factor and die together: under explicit conditions (among them, blanket answering loses score in expectation and prompts share a bounded readout) the model drifts toward refusing everything, its mean training reward rising to zero like $1/t$ in training time $t$, so the curve reads as improvement while coverage collapses. The advantage estimator compounds the failure: in its sparse-answer regime, group normalization silently replaces every designed penalty with an effective penalty of one, moving the learned threshold from $t^\ast$ to $1/2$. The repair is structural: train a mandatory confidence report with a strictly proper score plus a correctness reward, and abstain only at deployment by thresholding the report. The always-emitted report has no gate to saturate, so no shared factor can kill its reward gradient and its anchor together, and its calibrated optimum is attracting. Simulations confirm every prediction, and experiments on language models at two scales confirm the mechanism live: the rule silences questions the models demonstrably still solve within ten optimizer steps, an ablation isolates the cause, and report-level training raises coverage, accuracy, and calibration together.

</details>


### [96] [Agentic Bayesian Optimization through Surrogate-Augmented Autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00316)
*Paul Brunzema,Louis Tiao,Nhat Le,Kevin De Angeli,Yao Xuan,Djordje Gligorijevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体贝叶斯优化"（agentic BO）范式，其中LLM智能体作为BO循环中的核心决策者，由贝叶斯后端提供不确定性感知的优化基础。作者实现了Sara（代理增强的自动研究智能体）和lenz（模块化的BoTorch后端），在合成和真实基准上证明了该方法在保持标准BO可靠性的同时，超越LLM基线和标准BO，并能利用自然语言先验提升性能，还支持动态设置中实时重构优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化依靠通用的统计先验实现样本高效优化，但更丰富的领域先验难以通过定制核函数或问题结构编码到BO中。现有LLM-based BO方法要么将LLM固定在某一个角色，要么给予其控制权而牺牲BO的系统性探索可靠性。需要一种范式让LLM利用自然语言、代码和文档中的非正式先验，同时保持BO的可靠性。

Method: 提出agentic BO范式：LLM智能体作为BO循环中的核心决策者，贝叶斯后端提供不确定性感知优化基础。智能体负责配置问题、查询后端、选择和提交评估，并能在运行中修改优化策略（收紧边界、切换采集函数、提出针对性评估、重构问题）。具体实现为Sara（代理增强自动研究智能体）和lenz（模块化BoTorch后端，通过结构化接口供智能体检查和修改）。

Result: 在合成和真实基准上，Sara无需先验知识即可保持最先进BO的可靠性，超越LLM-based基线，并利用自然语言先验超越标准BO。在动态设置中，Sara能根据需求变化实时重构完整优化问题，这是标准BO此前不具备的能力。

Conclusion: agentic BO将LLM的灵活推理能力与BO的不确定性感知系统探索相结合，既能利用丰富的非正式领域先验，又能保持优化过程的可靠性，并在动态场景中展现出传统BO无法实现的任务重构能力。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) has become the standard tool for sample-efficient optimization and owes its efficiency to uncertainty-aware search driven by generic statistical priors. Richer domain priors can improve BO in principle, but encoding them through tailored kernels or problem structure is difficult and rarely done in practice. LLMs can help sidestep this difficulty by making informal priors from natural language, code, and documentation directly available to the optimizer. However, existing LLM-based BO methods either insert the LLM into a fixed role (surrogate, acquisition proxy, or configuration interface) or hand it broad control, sacrificing the systematic exploration that makes BO reliable. We introduce agentic Bayesian optimization: a paradigm in which an LLM agent is the central decision maker in the BO loop while a Bayesian backend provides the uncertainty-aware optimization substrate. The agent configures the problem, queries the backend, selects and commits evaluations, and can revise the optimization strategy during the run by tightening bounds, switching acquisition functions, proposing targeted evaluations, or even reframing the problem following new instructions or observed evidence. We instantiate this idea in Sara, a surrogate-augmented autoresearch agent, and lenz, a modular BoTorch-based backend that the agent can inspect and modify through a structured interface. Across synthetic and real-world benchmarks, Sara preserves the reliability of state-of-the-art BO without prior knowledge, outperforms LLM-based baselines, and uses natural-language priors to improve beyond standard BO. We further demonstrate the practical value of agentic BO in dynamic settings, where Sara reconfigures the full optimization problem on the fly as requirements change, a capability not previously available in standard BO.

</details>


### [97] [Neural operator learning for collision-aware trajectory planning of spacecraft swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00320)
*Sidhdharth D. Sikka,Suyi Gao,Zehui Lu,Rongjie Lai,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种置换等变神经算子，可将航天器群、目标和碎片分布映射为整个星群的碰撞感知轨迹，并通过批量高斯-牛顿精化确保轨道动力学精确性。该算子无需最优轨迹标签训练，结合自监督物理目标与对抗威胁生成。在10个航天器上训练后，可零样本泛化到1000个航天器及超过11000个编目目标的场景，匹配单智能体最优控制求解器精度，同时规避最坏情况威胁并减少星群内近距离接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典轨迹优化方法因成对安全约束随星群规模增加而扩展性差，学习式规划器则难以跨星群规模和碎片密度泛化，亟需一种可扩展且泛化能力强的星群轨迹规划方法。

Method: 提出置换等变神经算子，在单次前向传播中将航天器、目标和碎片的分布映射为整个星群的碰撞感知轨迹；配合批量高斯-牛顿精化步骤保证精确轨道动力学；采用自监督物理目标与针对自身滚动生成的对抗威胁进行无标签训练。

Result: 在10个航天器上训练后零样本泛化至1000个航天器及11000余编目目标场景，精度匹配单智能体最优控制求解器，能够规避碎片盲基线无法应对的最坏情况威胁，并将星群内近距离接触减少数倍。

Conclusion: 基于物理的算子学习为拥挤轨道中的最优控制提供了一种快速、可扩展的替代方案，展示了在复杂轨道环境中高效规划大型航天器星群轨迹的潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft swarms must plan fuel-efficient, collision-free maneuvers in increasingly congested orbits, yet classical trajectory optimization scales poorly as pairwise safety constraints multiply with swarm size, and learning-based planners rarely transfer across swarm sizes or debris densities. Here we introduce a permutation-equivariant neural operator that maps distributions of spacecraft, targets and debris to collision-aware trajectories for an entire swarm in a single forward pass, paired with a batched Gauss-Newton finish that enforces exact orbital dynamics. The operator is trained without optimal-trajectory labels, combining self-supervised physics objectives with adversarial threats generated against its own rollouts. Trained on ten spacecraft, it generalizes zero-shot to swarms of 1,000 amid more than 11,000 catalogued objects, matching a per-agent optimal-control solver's accuracy, evading worst-case threats that a debris-blind baseline cannot, and reducing proximity within the swarm several-fold. Physics-grounded operator learning thus offers a fast, scalable alternative to optimal control for crowded orbits.

</details>


### [98] [Ensemble of Unsupervised Deep Learning for Clustering Imbalanced Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00346)
*Pulock Das,Yina Hou,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据不平衡下深度聚类方法在表格数据上的表现，提出了两种新的聚类集成方法（基于嵌入维度聚合和基于多数投票），在16个二分类表格数据集上验证了集成方法在ACC、NMI和ARI指标上优于单个聚类方法，并指出深度聚类可作为不平衡数据场景下监督分类的强替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据不平衡在监督分类中会导致多数类偏差，造成假阴性和准确率高估。深度聚类由于无需类标签进行表示学习，可能对类别不平衡具有免疫力，但其在不平衡条件下的表现尚未被系统研究。

Method: 1) 系统评估多种SOTA深度聚类方法在不同不平衡水平下的性能；2) 提出两种集成方法：一种在不同嵌入维度上聚合深度聚类分配结果，另一种对性能最优的聚类算法结果进行多数投票；3) 在16个二分类表格数据集上人工诱导不同水平的不平衡进行实验。

Result: 在不同不平衡水平下，不同深度聚类方法展现出各自的优势。集成方法在ACC、NMI和ARI指标上平均优于单个聚类方法，对数据不平衡具有更强的鲁棒性，能够更好地在无监督条件下识别真实类别。

Conclusion: 在不平衡数据场景下，深度聚类可以作为监督分类的强有力替代方案，集成方法进一步提升了无监督聚类的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Data imbalance poses a major challenge in supervised classification, where the majority-class bias contributes to false negatives and overestimates classification accuracy. Unsupervised deep clustering can be immune to class imbalance because representation learning for clustering is performed without class labels. Deep clustering has been proposed for images, languages, and graphs, while its application to tabular data has only emerged recently. This paper is among the first to examine the performance of state-of-the-art deep clustering methods under varying levels of data imbalance. We introduce two novel cluster ensemble approaches: one aggregates deep clustering assignments across different embedding dimensions, and the other applies majority voting to the best-performing clustering algorithms. Experiments on 16 binary tabular datasets with varying and artificially induced levels of imbalance reveal distinct strengths of different deep clustering methods. On average, our ensemble methods outperform individual clustering methods in ACC, NMI, and ARI scores, offering greater resilience to data imbalance when identifying ground-truth classes without supervision. Therefore, in an imbalanced data scenario, deep clustering can serve as a strong alternative to supervised classification.

</details>


### [99] [Modeling Unknown Nonlocal PDE Systems via Flow Map Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00400)
*Zhongshu Xu,Ying Li,Yanzhi Zhang,Dongbin Xiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种流映射学习（FML）框架，用于直接从解数据中建模未知的非局部偏微分方程，通过学习有限时间演化算子而非非局部算子本身，在一维和二维分数阶扩散及波动方程上实现了精确且稳定的长时间预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部偏微分方程在众多应用中频繁出现，但由于非局部算子的存在，其建模和学习通常非常困难。

Method: 提出流映射学习（FML）框架，直接学习有限时间演化算子而非近似底层的非局部算子；针对谱表示和网格表示分别开发了两种互补的公式化方法（模态空间与节点空间）。

Result: 在一维和二维分数阶扩散方程及波动方程的数值实验中，仅使用短观测窗口即可实现准确且稳定的长期预测。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为学习未知非局部动力学提供了一种有效的数据驱动框架，且无需显式计算非局部算子。

Abstract: Nonlocal partial differential equations arise in many applications but are often difficult to model and learn because of the presence of nonlocal operators. We present a flow-map learning (FML) framework for modeling unknown nonlocal PDEs directly from solution data. Rather than learning or approximating the underlying nonlocal operators, the proposed approach learns the finite-time evolution operator in either modal or nodal space. Two complementary formulations are developed for spectral and grid-based solution representations. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional fractional diffusion and wave equations demonstrate accurate and stable long-time prediction using only short observation windows. The proposed approach provides an effective data-driven framework for learning unknown nonlocal dynamics without explicit evaluation of nonlocal operators.

</details>


### [100] [DSETA: A Dual-Stage Continual Learning Framework for Travel Time Prediction in Dynamic Traffic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00402)
*Yanming Lyu,Yue Cheng,Lingkun Li,Ruipeng Gao,Xinyue Liu,Hui Gao,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DSETA框架，一个增量更新的双阶段ETA预测框架，通过将持续学习分为日内（intra-day）和日间（inter-day）两个阶段，分别适应短期交通波动和长期分布偏移，并引入历史交通知识整合模块防止灾难性遗忘，已在滴滴生产环境部署，在三个城市取得MAE降低效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETA预测方法要么无法适应不规则交通模式和突发拥堵，要么在处理新数据分布时未能解耦长期趋势与短期波动，导致在真实场景中性能下降，亟需一种能适应动态交通模式的持续学习框架。

Method: 提出DSETA双阶段增量更新框架：(1) 日内学习阶段，完全依赖实时数据，动态适应短期交通模式（如节假日、事故）；(2) 日间学习阶段，利用短时间窗口聚合的历史数据，捕获长期分布偏移知识（如季节趋势、路网演化）；(3) 历史交通知识整合模块，防止灾难性遗忘并保留常规模式知识。

Result: 在北京、武汉、西安三个城市的在线A/B测试中分别实现MAE降低6.62%、0.73%和2.40%；已成功部署于滴滴生产环境，每日处理数亿级别请求。

Conclusion: DSETA框架通过日内与日间两阶段持续学习及知识整合机制，有效解决了ETA预测中短期波动适应与长期趋势保持之间的平衡问题，在工业级场景中验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Estimated Time of Arrival (ETA) prediction is a core component of intelligent transportation systems. As traffic congestion patterns become increasingly dynamic in large cities, maintaining high prediction accuracy poses a major challenge for ride-hailing platforms. Existing methods either fail to adapt to irregular traffic patterns and sudden congestion, or suffer from new distributions without disentangling long-term trends from short-term fluctuations, thereby degrading model performance in real-world scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose DSETA, an incrementally updated Dual-Stage ETA prediction framework. Specifically, the continual learning process is divided into \textit{inter-day} and \textit{intra-day} stages. We first design the \textit{intra-day} learning stage, which relies entirely on real-time data to enable dynamic adaptation to short-term traffic patterns caused by events like holidays or accidents. Next, we develop the \textit{inter-day} learning stage, which leverages aggregated historical data from a short time window to capture knowledge of long-term distribution shifts, such as seasonal trends and traffic network evolution. Subsequently, to prevent catastrophic forgetting and preserve knowledge of regular patterns, we explore a \textit{Historical Traffic Knowledge Consolidation} module. Finally, we validate DSETA's effectiveness and robustness through extensive offline and online experiments conducted on real-world datasets from DiDi's platform. Online A/B tests across three major cities including Beijing, Wuhan, and Xi'an consistently demonstrated performance gains, achieving MAE reductions of 6.62\%, 0.73\%, and 2.40\% respectively. This framework has been successfully deployed in DiDi's production environment, processing hundreds of millions of daily requests and validating its strong performance in industrial applications.

</details>


### [101] [Unleashing the Potential of Large Language Models: A Blueprint for Real-Time, Enterprise-Ready Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00419)
*Muhammad Faizan Raza,Shuo,Yang,Satish Mahadevan Srinivasan,Joanna F. DeFranco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的、模式驱动的LLMOps架构，将实时数据摄取、持续学习、RAG和人工反馈整合到单一操作流程中，以解决大语言模型在实时、受监管场景中的知识陈旧、灾难性遗忘、幻觉和反馈循环薄弱等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型在实时、受监管的环境中面临知识过时、灾难性遗忘、幻觉和反馈循环薄弱等关键挑战，现有方案缺乏将这些能力统一整合的操作性架构。

Method: 提出四项核心贡献：1）AIPO自适应摄取模式编排器（基于FreshStreamBench评估）；2）STAR+FAR持续学习方法，包含稀疏时间适配器路由和新鲜度感知重放；3）SAGE，一种SLO感知的自适应检索策略，通过预测每查询的段落预算来满足尾部延迟目标；4）自动化的反馈驱动收敛阶段，带有RLHF触发机制。

Result: 该架构减少了延迟-成本-准确性之间的权衡，同时支持可审计性和回滚能力，适用于医疗和金融等高危行业。

Conclusion: 该统一LLMOps架构通过模式驱动的设计，在满足监管合规要求的同时有效平衡了延迟、成本与准确性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Large language models deployed in real-time, regulated settings face knowledge staleness, catastrophic forgetting, hallucination, and weak feedback loops. We present a unified, pattern-driven LLMOps architecture integrating real-time data ingestion, continual learning, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and human-in-the-loop feedback into a single operational pipeline. Four contributions map to established software design patterns: an adaptive ingestion pattern orchestrator (AIPO) evaluated with FreshStreamBench; STAR+FAR continual learning with sparse temporal adapter routing and freshness-aware replay; SAGE, an SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy predicting a per-query passage budget to meet tail-latency targets; and an automated feedback-driven convergence stage with RLHF triggers. The result reduces latency-cost-accuracy trade-offs while supporting auditability and rollback for high-risk sectors such as health care and finance.

</details>


### [102] [HP-JEPA: Hierarchical Partitioning for Multi-Resolution Graph Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00491)
*Ruichen Xu,Jingxiang Qu,Wenhan Gao,Jiaxing Zhang,Linsey Pang,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Yann LeCun,Yuefan Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出HP-JEPA，一种用于图自监督学习的分层多分辨率联合嵌入预测框架，通过在不同粒度下进行上下文-目标潜在空间预测并整合各分辨率表示，解决现有图JEPA方法依赖单一固定图划分、难以捕获多尺度结构信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图JEPA方法通常依赖单一预定义的图划分，导致学习到的表示偏向单一结构粒度，无法充分捕获图数据在不同尺度上的互补模式（局部、区域、全局），限制了表示的可迁移性和表达能力。

Method: HP-JEPA将每个图组织成从粗到细的有序多分辨率图划分库，在每个分辨率下独立执行上下文-目标潜在表示预测，使用在线编码器、指数移动平均目标编码器和潜在预测器；各分辨率特定的图表示通过拼接或任务特定的分辨率加权方式进行整合。

Result: 在7个图分类基准和1个图回归基准上的实验表明，HP-JEPA在8个任务中的6个上优于固定分辨率的Graph-JEPA基线；在三个代表性数据集上的按图大小分层分析中，HP-JEPA在大多数图大小四分位数中取得了更高的准确率。

Conclusion: 分层多分辨率图划分能有效提升图表示学习的迁移性能，验证了整合互补的局部、区域和全局结构信息对于可迁移图表示学习的重要性。

Abstract: Graph self-supervised learning aims to learn transferable representations from large-scale unlabeled graph data. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) avoid explicit negative-pair construction and raw-input reconstruction by predicting masked targets directly in latent space. However, existing graph JEPAs typically rely on a single predefined graph partition, biasing the learned representations toward one structural granularity and limiting their ability to capture complementary patterns at different graph scales. To address this limitation, we propose HP-JEPA, a hierarchical partitioning framework for multi-resolution graph joint-embedding prediction. HP-JEPA organizes each graph into an ordered bank of coarse-to-fine partition resolutions and performs context-target latent prediction separately at each resolution using an online encoder, an exponential-moving-average target encoder, and a latent predictor. The resulting resolution-specific graph representations are subsequently integrated through concatenation or task-specific resolution weighting, allowing downstream models to combine complementary local, regional, and global structural information. Experiments on seven graph classification benchmarks and one graph regression benchmark show that HP-JEPA outperforms the fixed-resolution Graph-JEPA baseline on 6 of 8 tasks, improving upon Graph-JEPA on most evaluated benchmarks. Size-stratified analyses further show that HP-JEPA achieves higher accuracy than Graph-JEPA in most evaluated graph-size quartiles on three representative datasets. These results highlight the effectiveness of hierarchical multi-resolution partitioning for transferable graph representation learning.

</details>


### [103] [Agentic Graph Token Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00542)
*Zhuoyi Peng,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"智能体图令牌推理"方法，将图令牌编码纳入大语言模型的推理过程本身，模型在每一步自主选择要编码的图视图和粒度，图编码器按需生成图令牌并拼接进上下文，实现轨迹依赖的逐步图推理。通过三阶段训练流程（自监督图令牌阅读、令牌鲁棒轨迹训练、偏好优化），在七个图领域上大幅超越基线，并实现零样本跨域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图令牌的LLM图分析方法采用静态一次性方式：在模型看到目标之前就编码一个预定义的图视图且从不修正，未能利用模型逐步推理的能力。作者希望将图令牌化重塑为推理过程本身的一部分，让模型能够动态选择和调整图视图。

Method: 提出智能体图令牌推理框架，模型每步自主选择图视图及粒度，图编码器按需生成图令牌并拼接进运行上下文。采用三阶段训练管道：(i)自监督任务教授模型阅读异构图令牌；(ii)带图令牌一致性正则化的令牌鲁棒轨迹阶段；(iii)偏好优化奖励图令牌证据与节点文本证据一致的轨迹。

Result: 在七个图领域的评估中，模型大幅超越广泛基线，并能零样本迁移到未见领域而无需针对目标的微调。

Conclusion: 该工作将基于LLM的图分析从静态图令牌编码器推向图原生智能体范式，验证了图令牌作为推理过程组成部分的有效性。

Abstract: Graphs model relational data throughout science and industry, from citation networks to product co-purchase graphs. Because the nodes of many such graphs carry rich text, a growing line of work applies large language models (LLMs) to graph analysis. The most graph-native of these methods use graph tokens: a graph encoder compresses a graph view, such as a node, its k-hop neighbourhood, or a cluster, into a short block of continuous tokens that jointly encodes node attributes and topology and is read directly by the model. Existing methods, however, use graph tokens in a static single-shot manner: they encode one predefined graph view before the model has even seen the target and never revise it, leaving the model's step-by-step reasoning ability unused. We introduce agentic graph token reasoning, which recasts graph tokenization as part of the reasoning process itself. At each step, the model chooses which graph view to encode and at what granularity; a graph encoder is invoked on demand to materialise the corresponding graph tokens; and the resulting block is spliced into the running context. The model thus reasons step by step in the graph token space, and the tokens it reads are trajectory-dependent. We realise this with a three-stage training pipeline: (i) self-supervised tasks that teach the model to read heterogeneous graph tokens, (ii) a token-robust trajectory stage with a graph-token consistency regulariser, and (iii) preference optimisation that rewards trajectories in which the graph-token evidence and the node-text evidence agree. Across evaluations spanning seven graph domains, our models outperform a broad set of baselines by a large margin and transfer zero-shot to unseen domains without any per-target fine-tuning. More broadly, this work pushes LLM-based graph analysis from static graph-token encoders towards a graph-native agent paradigm.

</details>


### [104] [Crushing the Evidence: A Dual-Penalty Evasion Framework for Fooling White-Box Explainable AI Auditors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00566)
*Niraj Kumar,Harsh Kasyap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种白盒、梯度正则化的对抗性解释攻击框架，通过连续嵌入双惩罚机制直接在训练阶段对触发特征梯度施加惩罚，将规避逻辑原生嵌入模型参数中，从而在不依赖OOD包装器的情况下生成无异常足迹的平滑分布内预测，成功绕过解释器审计并掩盖算法偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性解释攻击依赖OOD检测器的脚手架包装，会留下可被防御系统识别的异常扰动足迹。作者旨在填补这一空白，证明存在更隐蔽的攻击方式——白盒、梯度正则化的规避攻击，能从根本上绕过现有防御机制。

Method: 提出一种白盒、梯度正则化的逃避攻击框架，采用连续嵌入双惩罚（dual-penalty）机制，在分布内数据的训练过程中直接对触发特征梯度进行惩罚。该方法将规避逻辑原生嵌入模型参数中，无需依赖OOD脚手架包装器，从而生成平滑的分布内预测且不留下异常足迹。

Result: 在四个基准表格数据集（COMPAS、German Credit、IEEE-CIS和Communities & Crime）上的实证评估表明：该方法能将目标特征归因压制到接近零（<0.02），维持超过90%的攻击成功率，并从根本上绕过条件异常检测（Conditional Anomaly Detection）。

Conclusion: 现有基于异常足迹检测的防御方法无法抵御此类白盒、梯度正则化的对抗性解释攻击，说明后验可解释性管道存在根本性的安全漏洞，需要开发更强大的防御策略来保障高风险领域模型审计的可靠性与透明度。

Abstract: Post-hoc model explainers such as LIME, SHAP, and Integrated Gradients are widely deployed to audit models in high-stakes sensitive domains, including finance, healthcare, and social welfare. This ensures the model's transparency and acceptability. However, a few studies have examined potential attacks in the explainability pipeline. Adversaries can attempt to conceal algorithmic biases or backdoors using adversarial explanation attacks. These attacks have relied on scaffolding out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors that toggle predictions when queried by an explainer. Consequently, defenses have been developed to successfully neutralize these black-box attacks by identifying their anomalous perturbation footprints. In this paper, we demonstrate a critical vulnerability by introducing a more potent white-box, gradient-regularized evasion attack framework. By employing a continuous-embedding dual-penalty framework, we directly penalize trigger feature gradients during training on in-distribution data. Since our approach embeds the evasion logic natively into the model parameters, without relying on OOD scaffolding wrappers, it generates smooth, in-distribution predictions that leave no anomaly footprint. Empirical evaluations across four benchmark tabular datasets (COMPAS, German Credit, IEEE-CIS, and Communities & Crime) confirm that our method systematically crushes target feature attribution to near-zero (<0.02), maintains >90% Attack Success Rates, and fundamentally bypasses Conditional Anomaly Detection.

</details>


### [105] [Fairness Auditing: Lower Bounds on Company Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00568)
*Rachit Verma,Padala Manisha,Sujit Gujar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限审计资源下公平性审计的固有局限，通过最小-最大优化框架量化了审计后不可规避的操纵程度，推导出了审计预算、群体不平衡和公平性容忍度与最坏情况人口统计平价偏差之间的显式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证明黑盒公平性审计存在不可回避的不可能结果，但缺乏对有限审计资源下审计后操纵空间的量化分析，作者旨在补充这一理论空白。

Method: 将公平性审计建模为计算能力无界的公司与预算受限的审计者之间的最小-最大优化问题，研究两种审计体制（固定大小审计集和α容忍审计集），并推导最坏情况下的人口统计平价偏差下界，辅以线性模型和神经网络分类器的实证验证。

Result: 推导出两种审计体制下最坏情况审计后操纵下界的显式表达式；实证结果表明增加审计资源虽能降低操纵空间，但无法完全消除，验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 有限预算的公平性认证存在根本性局限：增加审计资源只能缓解而不能消除审计后操纵，这凸显了当前公平性审计框架的理论上限。

Abstract: Fairness audits are increasingly mandated in high-stakes applications such as hiring, lending, and automated decision-making. Recent work has established fundamental impossibility results for black-box fairness auditing, showing that sufficiently expressive models can evade any auditing strategy. We complement these results by quantifying the extent of unavoidable post-audit manipulation under finite audit resources. We formulate fairness auditing as a min-max optimization between a computationally unbounded company and a budget-constrained auditor. We study two auditing regimes: (i) a budgeted auditor that certifies fairness using a fixed-size audit set, and (ii) a budgeted α-tolerant auditor that additionally requires the audit set to estimate the fairness of the certified model within an α approximation. For both settings, we derive explicit lower bounds on the worst-case post-audit demographic parity deviation as functions of the audit budget, group imbalance, and fairness tolerance. Finally, we empirically illustrate these theoretical limits using simple audit-set construction heuristics with linear and neural network classifiers. Our results demonstrate that increasing audit resources reduces, but does not eliminate, the scope for post-audit manipulation, highlighting fundamental limitations of finite-budget fairness certification.

</details>


### [106] [CoSynFlow: Conformal Symplectic Neural Flows for Cross-System Prediction of Dissipative Hamiltonian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00571)
*Baige Xu,Takaharu Yaguchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSynFlow，一种共形辛神经流，用于学习耗散哈密顿动力学的连续时间解算子。该方法通过组合辛剪切映射和显式共形缩放，在构造上保持共形辛结构，并可通过有限维哈密顿描述符和耗散参数进行条件化，实现跨系统泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子方法在求解微分方程时大多只优化预测精度，未显式强制动力学的几何结构。SympNets和辛神经流等结构保持方法仅针对保守哈密顿系统，对于具有共形辛结构的耗散哈密顿系统尚未有解决方案。

Method: CoSynFlow通过组合辛剪切映射与显式共形缩放来构造网络，在构造层面上保持共形辛结构。网络以有限维哈密顿描述符和耗散参数为条件输入，使单一训练模型能够预测未见系统的解映射。

Result: CoSynFlow将结构误差保持在机器精度水平，在长时程预测中取得最低误差，并支持物理信息训练。

Conclusion: CoSynFlow有效填补了耗散哈密顿系统中结构保持神经算子研究的空白，在保持共形辛几何结构的同时实现了高精度长时程预测和跨系统泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning solution operators for differential equations is a central problem in scientific machine learning. However, many neural operator methods optimize prediction accuracy without explicitly enforcing the geometric structure of the dynamics. Structure-preserving models such as SympNets and Symplectic Neural Flows address this issue for conservative Hamiltonian systems by preserving the symplectic form. In dissipative Hamiltonian systems with conformal symplectic structure, however, the symplectic form evolves according to a conformal factor determined by the dissipation. We propose CoSynFlow, a conformal symplectic neural flow for learning continuous-time solution maps of dissipative Hamiltonian dynamics. CoSynFlow composes symplectic shear maps with explicit conformal scaling, preserving the conformal symplectic structure by construction. By conditioning it on a finite-dimensional Hamiltonian descriptor and the dissipation parameter, a single trained model predicts solution maps for unseen systems without retraining. CoSynFlow keeps the structure error at machine precision, attains the lowest long-horizon error, and admits physics-informed training.

</details>


### [107] [From field-scale to large-scale spectral libraries: Tabular foundation models in soil spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00608)
*Viacheslav Barkov,Jonas Schmidinger,Robin Gebbers,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了多种回归模型（TabPFN、CNN、Cubist、随机森林、PLSR）和降维方法（PCA、PLS）在85个土壤光谱回归任务中的表现，发现TabPFN在完整光谱上即超越所有传统基线，且PLS降维结合TabPFN获得最佳预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见-近红外和中红外光谱可实现快速低成本的土壤属性预测，但高维、强共线性的光谱数据在机器学习建模中仍具挑战，缺乏系统性的模型与降维方法比较指导。

Method: 在85个来自开放基准数据集的回归任务上（涵盖田间尺度数字土壤制图和全球土壤光谱库），系统比较TabPFN、CNN、Cubist、随机森林和PLSR五种模型，并考察PCA和PLS潜在变量两种降维策略的组合效果。

Result: TabPFN在所有尺度上均取得最佳整体性能，直接应用于完整光谱即超越所有经典基线；PLS被证明是有效的降维策略，PLS潜在变量结合TabPFN获得最佳整体预测效果。

Conclusion: 研究为光谱校准模型选择提供了基于证据的跨尺度指导，表明PLSR的长期优势和现代表格基础模型在化学计量学中具有互补性。

Abstract: Visible and near-infrared (vis-NIR) and mid-infrared (MIR) spectroscopy enable rapid, cost-effective prediction of soil properties. Yet, translating high-dimensional, highly collinear spectra into accurate soil property predictions remains challenging, particularly when employing machine learning. We systematically investigated regression models and dimensionality reduction approaches for spectroscopic modeling across 85 regression tasks from open benchmark datasets in pedometrics spanning field-scale digital soil mapping and a global soil spectral library. We compared an in-context learning tabular foundation model (TabPFN), a convolutional neural network (CNN), rule-based regression (Cubist), Random Forest, and partial least squares regression (PLSR) using full spectra as well as features derived from principal component analysis (PCA) and partial least squares (PLS) latent variables. TabPFN consistently delivered the best overall performance across scales, including large spectral library tasks with tens of thousands of soil samples. Notably, TabPFN applied directly to full spectra already surpassed all classical baselines, showing that explicit dimensionality reduction is not strictly required for strong performance. Further improvements were achieved through PLS, which proved to be an effective dimensionality reduction strategy for all models. Combining PLS latent variables with TabPFN yielded the best predictions overall. Our findings provide evidence-based guidance for spectroscopic calibration model selection across operational scales, demonstrating that the long-standing advantages of PLSR and modern tabular foundation models complement each other in chemometrics.

</details>


### [108] [RHEA: Reliability-Harmonized Reconstruction and Assignment for Robust Multimodal-Attributed Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00621)
*Yinlin Zhu,Di Wu,Ziyu Han,Zekai Chenm,Wang Luo,Miao Hu,Guocong Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RHEA框架，用于多模态属性图（MAG）聚类，解决节点级模态可靠性差异导致的聚类性能退化问题。RHEA从图邻域中估计节点特定的模态可靠性，重构不可靠或缺失的模态，并在聚类全流程中传播可靠性信号，在四个基准数据集和五种属性条件下均优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAG聚类方法在属性干净完整时能有效融合互补模态，但在属性含噪或缺失时性能大幅下降，因为它们隐含假设所有节点的模态可靠性相同。而实际中模态可靠性是节点特定的——图像可能损坏或缺失，文本描述可能不完整或含噪声。因此需要一种能感知节点级模态可靠性的聚类方法。

Method: 提出RHEA（Reliability-aware framework for MAG clustering）：1）从图邻域一致性中估计节点特定的模态可靠性；2）从图邻域重构不可靠或缺失的模态；3）在可靠性感知融合中自适应加权模态；4）执行拓扑感知最优传输聚类，包含可靠性感知传输分配和邻居一致性分配蒸馏；5）将重构表示的置信度纳入聚类目标函数，使不确定的重构按比例贡献。

Result: 在四个MAG基准数据集、五种属性条件下，RHEA一致优于最强基线，且NMI增益随属性质量恶化而增大，即在更恶劣的属性条件下优势更明显。

Conclusion: 利用属性同质性假设，图邻域可自然提供无监督的节点级模态可靠性估计信号，RHEA通过在全聚类流程中传播可靠性信号，有效应对噪声和缺失属性，大幅提升MAG聚类的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs), whose nodes carry heterogeneous attributes such as text and images over a relational structure, have become a fundamental substrate for label-free entity grouping tasks, including community discovery and product segmentation. Existing MAG clustering methods effectively integrate complementary modalities when attributes are clean and complete, but degrade substantially under noisy or missing attributes because they implicitly assume equal modality reliability across all nodes. In practice, modality reliability is inherently node-specific: images may be corrupted or absent, while textual descriptions are incomplete or noisy. We argue that, under attribute homophily, graph neighborhoods naturally provide supervision-free evidence for estimating node-specific modality reliability. Based on this insight, we propose RHEA, a reliability-aware framework for MAG clustering that estimates node-specific modality reliability from neighborhood consensus and propagates this signal throughout the clustering pipeline. RHEA reconstructs unreliable or missing modalities from graph neighborhoods, adaptively weights modalities during reliability-aware fusion, and performs topology-aware optimal transport clustering with reliability-aware transport assignment and neighbor-consensus assignment distillation. Furthermore, the confidence of reconstructed representations is incorporated into the clustering objective, allowing uncertain reconstructions to contribute proportionally during optimization. Experiments on four MAG benchmarks under five attribute conditions show that RHEA consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, with NMI gains increasing as attribute quality deteriorates.

</details>


### [109] [Towards Effective Federated Multimodal Graph Learning via Navigating Multifaceted Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00623)
*Yinlin Zhu,Di Wu,Yi Zhang,Xunkai Li,Wang Luo,Wei-Jin Huang,Miao Hu,Guocong Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedTCR，这是首个针对联邦多模态图学习（FMGL）的系统性算法，通过两阶段训练范式（联邦任务无关预训练+隔离任务微调）和拓扑感知的跨模态路由机制，联合解决任务异质性、模态异质性和拓扑异质性三大挑战，在7个领域上优于现有最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦图学习（FGL）方法直接应用于联邦多模态图学习（FMGL）时存在不足，无法应对分散式多模态图中固有的多重异质性挑战，包括任务异质性（不同客户端目标不同）、模态异质性（模态质量和语义域不一致）以及拓扑异质性（拓扑模式发散且跨模态相关性低）。

Method: FedTCR包含两个核心设计：(1) 两阶段范式：先进行联邦任务无关的预训练，再进行隔离的任务导向微调，以应对任务异质性；(2) 拓扑感知的跨模态路由机制：每个客户端通过图结构启发的拓扑感知重要性加权聚合，将模态特定知识蒸馏为紧凑原型；服务器评估跨客户端跨模态关系并路由信息丰富的原型作为对比参考，驱动三层级跨模态对比学习方案，联合对齐跨客户端模态同时保持判别性。

Result: 在7个领域上的实验表明，FedTCR在图中心任务和模态中心任务上均优于现有最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedTCR作为首个系统性FMGL算法，成功同时解决了任务、模态和拓扑三重异质性挑战，验证了拓扑感知的跨模态路由和两阶段训练范式在联邦多模态图学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs), where nodes carry heterogeneous semantic content across multiple modalities while edges encode relational dependencies, have been widely adopted across diverse domains. Federated multimodal graph learning (FMGL) extends federated graph learning (FGL) to MAGs, enabling collaborative optimization across decentralized MAGs without exposing raw data. However, naively applying existing FGL methods to FMGL is insufficient, as they fail to navigate the multifaceted heterogeneity inherent in decentralized MAGs, including task heterogeneity across diverse client objectives, modality heterogeneity from discrepant modality quality and semantic domains, and topology heterogeneity arising from divergent topological patterns with low cross-modality correlation. To address these challenges, we propose Federated multimodal graph learning with Topology-aware Cross-modal Routing (FedTCR), the first systematic algorithm designed for FMGL. To handle task heterogeneity, FedTCR employs a two-stage paradigm that comprises federated task-agnostic pre-training followed by isolated task-oriented fine-tuning. To jointly address modality and topology heterogeneity, FedTCR introduces a topology-aware cross-modal routing mechanism. Concretely, each client distills modality-specific knowledge into compact prototypes via topology-aware importance-weighted aggregation informed by graph structure; the server then evaluates cross-client cross-modal relationships among these structure-informed prototypes and routes informative ones as contrastive references, driving a tri-level cross-modal contrastive learning scheme that jointly aligns cross-client modalities while preserving discrimination. Experiments across 7 domains demonstrate that FedTCR outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on both graph-centric and modality-centric tasks.

</details>


### [110] [Relative Parameter Importance in Task-Agnostic Replay-Free Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00630)
*Malavika Suresh,Ikechukwu Nkisi-Orji,Nirmalie Wiratunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的度量指标"相对参数重要性"（relative parameter-importance），用于深度神经网络的持续学习（CL），在无先前任务数据和推理时无任务ID的约束下，通过区分参数对当前与过往任务的重要性来平衡稳定性与可塑性，同时支持向后的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习需要在稳定性与可塑性之间取得平衡，同时实现知识迁移。现有方法多关注防止灾难性遗忘，但往往牺牲了向后的知识迁移能力。本文旨在解决在严格约束下（无先前任务数据、推理时无任务ID）的离线持续学习问题。

Method: 引入"相对参数重要性"度量，衡量每个参数相对于当前和过往任务的重要性。高相对重要性的参数被视为对维持过往任务稳定性更关键，从而施加强正则化；低相对重要性的参数则允许更自由地更新。与现有方法不同，该方法允许更新具有高过往任务重要性但低相对重要性的参数，从而在解决稳定性-可塑性权衡的同时实现向后的知识迁移。

Result: 在类增量（class-incremental）和域增量（domain-incremental）文本分类问题上，方法优于现有最先进的持续学习方法，并为扩展到文本生成任务提供了见解。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于相对参数重要性的方法能够有效平衡稳定性和可塑性，同时实现向后的知识迁移，在文本分类的持续学习任务上取得了优于现有方法的性能，并具备向文本生成任务扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Achieving continual learning (CL) with deep neural networks requires balancing stability and plasticity while enabling knowledge transfer. In this work, we focus on offline learning algorithms under the constraints: (I) no access to training data from prior tasks (II) no access to task-id at inference time. We introduce a novel measure, the relative parameter-importance, which measures the relative importance of each parameter with respect to both the current and past tasks.
